- **clip-sports**: Cut sports footage or a match broadcast into vertical Shorts / Reels / TikToks. Finds goals, wickets, points, saves and finishes from crowd and commentator noise and from scoreboard changes, shows a scored table for approval, then renders edited 1080x1920 clips with a headline, punch-in and slow-motion on the key moment, and the real score as a callout. Use when the user runs /clip-sports, or gives a YouTube link or video file of a match, game or race and wants highlights, shorts or best moments. ([lowdevco/Clip-Forge](https://github.com/lowdevco/Clip-Forge/blob/HEAD/commands/clip-sports.md) · ★66)

- **clip-tutorial**: Cut a tutorial, lesson, walkthrough or explainer into vertical Shorts / Reels / TikToks. Finds complete single ideas (one tip, one fix, one result) from the transcript, shows a scored table for approval, then renders clean 1080x1920 clips with readable screen framing, captions and silences trimmed. Use when the user runs /clip-tutorial, or gives a YouTube link or video file of a how-to, course, coding or screen-recording video and wants shorts, tips or clips. ([lowdevco/Clip-Forge](https://github.com/lowdevco/Clip-Forge/blob/HEAD/commands/clip-tutorial.md) · ★66)
- **clip-video**: Cut any long video (vlog, tutorial, review, event, sports, travel) into vertical Shorts / Reels / TikToks. Finds the best moments from both the transcript and the pictures, shows a scored table for approval, then renders 1080x1920 clips with captions, a hook headline and loudness-normalised audio. Use when the user runs /clip-video, or gives a YouTube link or video file that is neither a podcast nor gameplay and wants shorts, clips or highlights. ([lowdevco/Clip-Forge](https://github.com/lowdevco/Clip-Forge/blob/HEAD/commands/clip-video.md) · ★66)

([turboBasic/uv-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/uv-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-cache.md) · ★1)
- **cmd-cat**: Writes the computed target contents of files, scripts, or symlinks to stdout. Useful for previewing what chezmoi would write without actually applying. ([turboBasic/chezmoi-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/chezmoi-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-cat.md))
- **cmd-cclear**: Clear stray and improper completions from Zinit's completions directory. Use this to fix broken or dangling completion symlinks. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-cclear.md))
- **cmd-cd**: Launches a subshell in the chezmoi working tree (source directory), optionally at the source path corresponding to a given target path. Useful for quick edits to source state files. ([turboBasic/chezmoi-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/chezmoi-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-cd.md))
- **cmd-cd**: Change the current directory to a plugin's local directory. Also supports snippets when given their URL. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-cd.md))
- **cmd-cdclear**: Clear the list of intercepted `compdef` calls, discarding any completions captured so far. Use this to ignore completions registered by plugins loaded before this point. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-cdclear.md))
- **cmd-cdisable**: Disable a specific installed completion without uninstalling it. The completion file remains on disk but is removed from the active `$fpath` symlinks. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-cdisable.md))
- **cmd-cdlist**: Show the list of `compdef` calls that have been intercepted and are waiting to be replayed. Use this to inspect what completions have been captured before `compinit` ran. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-cdlist.md))
- **cmd-cdreplay**: Replay all `compdef` calls that were intercepted by Zinit before `compinit` was available. Run this once after calling `compinit` to register completions that plugins tried to define earlier. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-cdreplay.md))
- **cmd-cenable**: Re-enable a completion that was previously disabled with `zi cdisable`. Restores the symlink so the completion is active for `compinit`. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-cenable.md))

- **cmd-chattr**: Changes the attributes and/or type of targets in the source state by renaming source files to add or remove attribute prefixes. This is the primary way to toggle properties like `executable`, `private`, `template`, `encrypted`, etc. ([turboBasic/chezmoi-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/chezmoi-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-chattr.md))
- **cmd-commit**: You are a git commit specialist that creates well-structured conventional commits following the standard format. When this command is run, you will: ([elijahmurray/vandra-dev](https://github.com/elijahmurray/vandra-dev/blob/HEAD/commands/cmd-commit.md) · ★2)
- **cmd-compile**: Compile a plugin's Zsh scripts to `.zwc` bytecode to speed up loading. Can target a single plugin or all managed plugins at once. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-compile.md))
- **cmd-compiled**: List all plugins that currently have compiled `.zwc` bytecode files. Use this to audit which plugins benefit from compilation. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-compiled.md))

- **cmd-completion**: - Bash: add to `.bashrc`; also requires the `bash-completion` package. - Zsh: add to `.zshrc`. - fish: add to your `.fish` file. - PowerShell: add to your `.ps1` file; requires script execution to be enabled. - **cmd-csearch**: Search all downloaded plugin directories for available completion files, whether or not those completions are currently installed. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-csearch.md))
- **cmd-cuninstall**: Uninstall (remove) completions for a plugin from Zinit's completions directory. The plugin itself is not deleted. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-cuninstall.md))
- **cmd-data**: Writes the computed template data to stdout in JSON or YAML format. Use this to inspect what variables are available in templates (`.chezmoi.*` and user-defined data). ([turboBasic/chezmoi-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/chezmoi-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-data.md))
- **cmd-dclear**: Clear the session investigation report without reverting any changes. Resets the tracking data accumulated since `zi dstart`. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-dclear.md))
- **cmd-delete**: Remove a plugin or snippet from disk. Use this to free disk space or to forget an incorrectly configured plugin so it can be cleanly re-installed. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-delete.md))
- **cmd-destroy**: Permanently removes a target from the source state, the destination directory, and the chezmoi state database. This is a destructive operation that deletes files from both your home directory and the source directory. ([turboBasic/chezmoi-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/chezmoi-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-destroy.md))
- **cmd-diff**: Prints the difference between the target state (what chezmoi wants) and the destination state (what currently exists on disk). Use this to preview what `chezmoi apply` would change. ([turboBasic/chezmoi-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/chezmoi-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-diff.md))
- **cmd-docs**: `dbt docs` has two supported subcommands: `generate` and `serve`. ([three-cubes/kairix](https://github.com/three-cubes/kairix/blob/HEAD/reference-library/data-and-analysis/dbt-docs/website/docs/reference/commands/cmd-docs.md) · ★1)
- **cmd-doctor**: Runs a series of checks to detect potential problems with the chezmoi installation, configuration, and environment. First command to run when troubleshooting. ([turboBasic/chezmoi-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/chezmoi-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-doctor.md))
- **cmd-doctor**: Displays diagnostic information about your mise installation and detects potential issues. Include its output in bug reports. ([turboBasic/mise-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/mise-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-doctor.md))
- **cmd-document** ([turboBasic/1password-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/1password-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-document.md))
- **cmd-dreport**: Print a report of everything that happened in the current interactive session between `dstart`/`dtrace` and `dstop`. Shows aliases, functions, bindkeys, and other changes made during the tracked period. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-dreport.md))
- **cmd-dstart**: Start investigating the current interactive session — Zinit begins recording all aliases, functions, bindkeys, and other shell-state changes from this point forward. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-dstart.md))
- **cmd-dstop**: Stop the active session investigation started with `zi dstart`/`zi dtrace`. No further changes are recorded after this point. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-dstop.md))
- **cmd-dump**: Dumps the full target state (type, permissions, contents, etc.) as JSON or YAML. Shows the complete metadata chezmoi has computed for targets, not just the file contents. ([turboBasic/chezmoi-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/chezmoi-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-dump.md))
- **cmd-dunload**: Revert all shell-state changes recorded between `zi dstart` and `zi dstop`. Undoes aliases, functions, bindkeys, and other modifications captured during the session investigation window. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-dunload.md))
- **cmd-edit**: Opens the source state of target files in your editor. Handles encrypted file decryption/re-encryption transparently and can optionally apply changes immediately after editing. ([turboBasic/chezmoi-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/chezmoi-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-edit.md))
- **cmd-edit**: Open a plugin's main file in `$EDITOR`. A quick way to inspect or patch a plugin's source without navigating to its directory manually. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-edit.md))
- **cmd-env**: Outputs environment variables that mise would set. Useful for debugging, non-interactive shells, and integration with other tools. ([turboBasic/mise-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/mise-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-env.md))
- **cmd-env-whitelist**: Specify environment variable names (or patterns) that should not be reverted when a plugin is unloaded. Use this to preserve variables that plugins set and that you want to keep after unloading. ([turboBasic/zinit-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/zinit-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-env-whitelist.md))
- **cmd-environment**: The `--environments` flag requires the latest beta build of 1Password CLI, version `2.33.0-beta.02` or later. ([turboBasic/1password-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/1password-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-environment.md))
- **cmd-events-api**: Flags for `events-api create`: ([turboBasic/1password-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/1password-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-events-api.md))
- **cmd-exec**: Execute a command with mise tool versions and environment variables loaded. The quickest way to run a tool at a specific version without activating mise in your shell. ([turboBasic/mise-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/mise-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-exec.md))
- **cmd-execute-template**: Executes template strings or files and prints the result to stdout. Essential for testing and debugging templates before committing them to the source state. ([turboBasic/chezmoi-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/chezmoi-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-execute-template.md))
- **cmd-export**: `uv export` converts `uv.lock` into an alternate format for use with other tools. It supports `requirements.txt` (pip-compatible), `pylock.toml` (PEP 751), and CycloneDX v1.5 JSON SBOM output. - **cmd-feature-document**: Document the feature by creating a specification and updating all relevant documentation files. ([elijahmurray/vandra-dev](https://github.com/elijahmurray/vandra-dev/blob/HEAD/commands/cmd-feature-document.md) · ★2)

- **cmd-generate**: Generates various outputs for use with chezmoi, including install scripts for GitHub Codespaces and git commit messages that describe source directory changes. ([turboBasic/chezmoi-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/chezmoi-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-generate.md))
- **cmd-git**: Runs `git` with the given arguments in the chezmoi working tree (source directory). A convenience wrapper so you do not need to `cd` into the source directory first. ([turboBasic/chezmoi-doc](https://github.com/turboBasic/chezmoi-doc/blob/HEAD/cards/commands/cmd-git.md))
